1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main loop: run `desloppify next`, fix what it says, resolve it, run `next` again. Over and over. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    19→
    20→**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
    21→
    22→## 2. The Workflow
    23→
    24→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    25→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    26→
    27→### Outer loop — scan and check
    28→
    29→```bash
    30→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    31→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    32→```
    33→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    34→
    35→### Inner loop — fix issues
    36→
    37→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    38→
    39→```
    40→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    41→2. Fix the issue in code
    42→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    43→```
    44→
    45→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    46→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    47→
    48→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    49→```bash
    50→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    51→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    52→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    53→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    54→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # skip / hide from next
    55→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    56→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    57→```
    58→
    59→### Subjective reviews
    60→
    61→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    62→If you need to trigger one manually:
    63→```bash
    64→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    65→```
    66→
    67→### Synthesis (after review)
    68→
    69→After a subjective review, `synthesis::pending` appears at the top of the queue.
    70→Complete the 4-stage workflow before moving to fixes:
    71→```bash
    72→desloppify plan synthesize                          # dashboard — see stage progress
    73→desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
    74→desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
    75→desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
    76→desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
    77→```
    78→
    79→### Other useful commands
    80→
    81→```bash
    82→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    83→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    84→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    85→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    86→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    87→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    88→```
    89→
    90→## 3. Reference
    91→
    92→### How scoring works
    93→
    94→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    95→
    96→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    97→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    98→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    99→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   100→
   101→### Subjective reviews in detail
   102→
   103→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   104→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   105→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   106→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   107→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   108→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   109→
   110→### Key concepts
   111→
   112→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   113→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   114→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   115→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   116→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   117→
   118→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   119→
   120→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   121→
   122→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   123→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   124→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   125→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   126→
   127→## Prerequisite
   128→
   129→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   130→
   131→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   132→
```

     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >

    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→4. desloppify next              ← get the next item; repeat
    46→```
    47→
    48→**Work the queue in order.** `next` returns items by priority — fix #1 first, then #1 again after resolving. Do NOT browse the list and cherry-pick easy items while skipping hard ones. If an item is genuinely blocked or wrong, use `plan move` to reorder or `plan skip` with a note — don't silently move on.
    49→
    50→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    51→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    52→
    53→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    54→```bash
    55→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    56→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    57→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    58→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    59→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next (requires --note for accountability)
    60→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    61→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    62→```
    63→
    64→**Anti-laziness rules:**
    65→- Never skip or defer items just because they are large or hard. Either fix them or use `plan move` to explicitly reorder with justification.
    66→- `plan skip` without `--permanent` still hides items — use it only for genuinely blocked work, not to avoid effort. Always include `--reason`.
    67→- `plan skip --permanent` accepts debt (widens strict gap) — requires `--note` and `--attest`.
    68→- Do not batch-browse with `next --count N` to cherry-pick. Run `next` (single item), fix it, resolve, repeat.
    69→
    70→### Subjective reviews
    71→
    72→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    73→If you need to trigger one manually:

    75→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    76→```

    78→### Other useful commands

    81→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    82→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    83→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    84→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    85→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    86→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    87→```
    88→
    89→## 3. Reference
    90→
    91→### How scoring works
    92→
    93→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    94→
    95→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    96→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    97→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    98→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    99→
   100→### Subjective reviews in detail
   101→
   102→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   103→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   104→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   105→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   106→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   107→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   108→
   109→### Key concepts
   110→
   111→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   112→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   113→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   114→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   115→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   116→
   117→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   118→
   119→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   120→
   121→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   122→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   123→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   124→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   125→
   126→## Prerequisite
   127→
   128→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   129→
   130→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   131→
   132→## Claude Code Overlay
   133→
   134→Use Claude subagents for subjective scoring work that should be context-isolated.
   135→
   136→### Subjective review
   137→
   138→1. **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   139→2. **Claude cloud path**: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` → use generated `claude_launch_prompt.md` + `review_result.template.json` → run printed `desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>`.
   140→3. **Manual path**: split dimensions across N subagents (one message, multiple Task calls), merge outputs, then `desloppify review --import findings.json`.
   141→
   142→For the manual path:
   143→- Read `dimension_prompts` from `query.json` for dimension definitions.
   144→- Give each agent the codebase path, dimensions, and output format. Let agents decide what to read.
   145→- Each agent writes output to a separate file. Merge assessments (average overlaps) and findings.
   146→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state for correlation.
   147→
   148→### Subagent rules
   149→
   150→1. Prefer delegating review tasks to a project subagent in `.claude/agents/`.
   151→2. Set `context: fork` so prior chat context does not leak into scoring.
   152→3. For blind reviews, consume `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` instead of full `query.json`.
   153→4. Score from evidence only; do not anchor to target thresholds. When mixed, score lower.
   154→5. Return machine-readable JSON matching the format in the base skill doc. For `--external-submit`, include `session` from the generated template.
   155→6. `findings` MUST match `query.system_prompt` exactly. Use `"findings": []` when no defects found.
   156→7. Import is fail-closed: invalid findings abort unless `--allow-partial` is passed.
   157→
   158→<!-- desloppify-overlay: claude -->
   159→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   160→

1→"""Reflect stage handler for plan synthesis."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import re
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis import shared as synth_shared_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.subjective_dimensions import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→from desloppify.core.output import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.synthesis import (
    13→    collect_synthesis_input,
    14→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    17→
    18→
    19→def _normalize_report_text(text: str) -> str:
    20→    return " ".join(str(text).strip().lower().replace("_", " ").split())
    21→
    22→
    23→def _dimension_report_aliases(dimension: str) -> set[str]:
    24→    aliases: set[str] = set()
    25→    canonical = _normalize_report_text(dimension)
    26→    if canonical:
    27→        aliases.add(canonical)
    28→    display_name = DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dimension, "")
    29→    display = _normalize_report_text(display_name)
    30→    if display:
    31→        aliases.add(display)
    32→    return aliases
    33→
    34→
    35→def _report_mentions_dimension(report: str, dimension: str) -> bool:
    36→    normalized_report = _normalize_report_text(report)
    37→    if not normalized_report:
    38→        return False
    39→    padded_report = f" {normalized_report} "
    40→    for alias in _dimension_report_aliases(dimension):
    41→        if alias and f" {alias} " in padded_report:
    42→            return True
    43→        if alias and re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(alias)}\b", normalized_report):
    44→            return True
    45→    return False
    46→
    47→
    48→def cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    49→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current findings against completed work."""
    50→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    51→
    52→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    53→    state = runtime.state
    54→    plan = load_plan()
    55→
    56→    if not synth_shared_mod._require_synthesis_pending(plan, action="reflect"):
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    60→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    61→    if "observe" not in stages:
    62→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    63→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    64→        return
    65→
    66→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    67→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
    68→
    69→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
    70→    validated_report = synth_shared_mod._validate_stage_report(
    71→        report,
    72→        stage="reflect",
    73→        min_chars=min_chars,
    74→        missing_guidance=[
    75→            "Compare current findings against completed work and form a holistic strategy:",
    76→            "- What clusters were previously completed? Did fixes hold?",
    77→            "- Are any dimensions recurring (resolved before, open again)?",
    78→            "- What contradictions did you find? Which direction will you take?",
    79→            "- Big picture: what to prioritize, what to defer, what to skip?",
    80→        ],
    81→        short_guidance=[
    82→            "Describe how current findings relate to previously completed work.",
    83→        ],
    84→    )
    85→    if validated_report is None:
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_findings, si.resolved_findings)
    89→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
    90→
    91→    if recurring_dims:
    92→        mentioned = [
    93→            dim
    94→            for dim in recurring_dims
    95→            if _report_mentions_dimension(validated_report, dim)
    96→        ]
    97→        if not mentioned:
    98→            print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
    99→            for dim in recurring_dims:
   100→                info = recurring[dim]
   101→                display = DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, dim.replace("_", " "))
   102→                print(
   103→                    colorize(
   104→                        f"    {display} ({dim}): {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   105→                        f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   106→                        "yellow",
   107→                    )
   108→                )
   109→            print(
   110→                colorize(
   111→                    "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
   112→                    "dim",
   113→                )
   114→            )
   115→            return
   116→
   117→    synth_shared_mod.record_synthesis_stage(
   118→        plan,
   119→        state,
   120→        stage="reflect",
   121→        report=validated_report,
   122→        cited_ids=[],
   123→        finding_count=finding_count,
   124→        extra={"recurring_dimensions": recurring_dims},
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    print(
   128→        colorize(
   129→            f"  Reflect stage recorded: {finding_count} findings, "
   130→            f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   131→            "green",
   132→        )
   133→    )
   134→    if recurring_dims:
   135→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   136→            info = recurring[dim]
   137→            print(
   138→                colorize(
   139→                    f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open",
   140→                    "dim",
   141→                )
   142→            )
   143→
   144→    print()
   145→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
   146→    for line in validated_report.strip().splitlines():
   147→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   148→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   149→    print()
   150→    print(
   151→        colorize(
   152→            "  IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:",
   153→            "yellow",
   154→        )
   155→    )
   156→    print(colorize("  - What themes and root causes you see", "yellow"))
   157→    print(
   158→        colorize(
   159→            "  - What contradictions you found and which direction you'll take",
   160→            "yellow",
   161→        )
   162→    )
   163→    print(
   164→        colorize(
   165→            "  - What you'll prioritize, what you'll defer, the overall arc of work",
   166→            "yellow",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→    print(colorize("  Wait for their input before creating clusters.", "yellow"))
   170→    print()
   171→    print(colorize("  Then create clusters and enrich each with action steps:", "dim"))
   172→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>", "dim"))
   174→    print(

1→"""Shared helpers for staged synthesis command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
     9→    SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    10→    SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
    11→    SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine.planning.synthesis import (
    15→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    16→    review_finding_snapshot_hash,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    19→from desloppify.engine.plan import SYNTHESIS_ID, save_plan
    20→
    21→
    22→def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    23→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display."""
    24→    if "::" in fid:
    25→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    26→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    27→            return suffix[:8]
    28→    return fid
    29→
    30→
    31→def _require_synthesis_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    32→    """Require ``synthesis::pending`` to be present in queue for an action."""
    33→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    34→        return True
    35→    print(colorize(f"  synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    36→    return False
    37→
    38→
    39→def _validate_stage_report(
    40→    report: str | None,
    41→    *,
    42→    stage: str,
    43→    min_chars: int,
    44→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    45→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    46→) -> str | None:
    47→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    48→    if not report:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    50→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    51→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    52→        return None
    53→    cleaned = report.strip()
    54→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    55→        print(
    56→            colorize(
    57→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    58→                "red",
    59→            )
    60→        )
    61→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    62→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    63→        return None
    64→    return cleaned
    65→
    66→
    67→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    68→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    69→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    70→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    71→    for c in clusters.values():
    72→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    73→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    74→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    75→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    76→
    77→
    78→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    79→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment."""
    80→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    81→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    82→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    83→            continue
    84→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    85→            continue
    86→        missing: list[str] = []
    87→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    88→            missing.append("description")
    89→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
    90→            missing.append("action_steps")
    91→        if missing:
    92→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    93→    return gaps
    94→
    95→
    96→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    97→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
    98→    return [
    99→        name
   100→        for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   101→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   106→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   107→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
   108→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   109→        if stage_name in stages:
   110→            print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label}", "green"))
   111→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   112→            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   113→        else:
   114→            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
   115→
   116→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   117→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   118→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   119→        if not manual:
   120→            print(
   121→                colorize(
   122→                    "\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.",
   123→                    "yellow",
   124→                )
   125→            )
   126→        elif gaps:
   127→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   128→            for name, missing in gaps:
   129→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   130→            print(
   131→                colorize(
   132→                    f"      Fix: {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
   133→                    "dim",
   134→                )
   135→            )
   136→        else:
   137→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   138→
   139→
   140→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_findings: dict) -> None:
   141→    """Show cluster state and unclustered findings."""
   142→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   143→    _print_active_clusters(clusters)
   144→    unclustered = _collect_unclustered_findings(clusters, open_findings)
   145→    _print_unclustered_findings(plan, open_findings, unclustered)
   146→
   147→
   148→def _print_active_clusters(clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   149→    """Print current clusters that contain findings."""
   150→    active_clusters = {name: c for name, c in clusters.items() if c.get("finding_ids")}
   151→    if not active_clusters:
   152→        return
   153→    print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
   154→    for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
   155→        count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   156→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   157→        tag_str = _cluster_tag_summary(cluster)
   158→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   159→        print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   160→
   161→
   162→def _cluster_tag_summary(cluster: dict) -> str:
   163→    """Build compact tag summary for one cluster row."""
   164→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   165→    auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
   166→    tags: list[str] = []
   167→    tags.append("auto" if auto else "manual")
   168→    tags.append("desc" if cluster.get("description") else "no desc")
   169→    if steps:
   170→        tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
   171→    elif not auto:
   172→        tags.append("no steps")
   173→    return f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
   174→
   175→
   176→def _collect_unclustered_findings(clusters: dict[str, dict], open_findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   177→    """Return finding IDs that are not attached to any cluster."""
   178→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   179→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   180→        all_clustered.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   181→    return [fid for fid in open_findings if fid not in all_clustered]
   182→
   183→
   184→def _print_unclustered_findings(
   185→    plan: dict,
   186→    open_findings: dict,
   187→    unclustered: list[str],
   188→) -> None:
   189→    """Print unclustered findings summary or all-clustered confirmation."""
   190→    if unclustered:
   191→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} findings not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   192→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   193→            finding = open_findings[fid]
   194→            dim = (
   195→                (finding.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "")
   196→                if isinstance(finding.get("detail"), dict)
   197→                else ""
   198→            )
   199→            short = _short_id(fid)
   200→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
   201→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   202→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   203→        return
   204→    if open_findings:
   205→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   206→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))
   207→
   208→
   209→def refresh_stage_snapshot(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   210→    """Mark stage progress as aligned with the current review-finding snapshot."""
   211→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   212→    meta["stage_snapshot_hash"] = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   213→    meta["stage_refresh_required"] = False
   214→
   215→
   216→def record_synthesis_stage(
   217→    plan: dict,
   218→    state: dict,
   219→    *,
   220→    stage: str,
   221→    report: str,
   222→    cited_ids: list[str],
   223→    finding_count: int,
   224→    extra: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   225→) -> dict[str, object]:
   226→    """Persist one stage payload, refresh snapshot metadata, and save the plan."""
   227→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   228→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   229→    payload: dict[str, object] = {
   230→        "stage": stage,
   231→        "report": report,
   232→        "cited_ids": list(cited_ids),
   233→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   234→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   235→    }
   236→    if extra:
   237→        payload.update(extra)
   238→    stages[stage] = payload
   239→    refresh_stage_snapshot(plan, state)
   240→    save_plan(plan)
   241→    return payload
   242→
   243→
   244→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   245→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   246→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   247→
   248→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   249→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   250→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   251→
   252→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   253→    state = runtime.state
   254→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   255→
   256→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   257→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   258→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   259→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   260→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   261→    )
   262→    meta["synthesized_ids"] = open_review_ids
   263→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   264→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   265→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   266→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   267→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   268→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   269→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   270→
   271→    save_plan(plan)
   272→
   273→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   274→    print(
   275→        colorize(
   276→            f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).",
   277→            "green",
   278→        )
   279→    )
   280→    effective_strategy = (
   281→        strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   282→    )
   283→    if effective_strategy:
   284→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   285→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   286→
   287→
   288→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   289→    """Show completed clusters, resolved findings, and recurring patterns."""
   290→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   291→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_findings", {})
   292→    open_findings = getattr(si, "open_findings", {})
   293→
   294→    _print_completed_clusters(completed)
   295→    _print_resolved_findings(resolved)
   296→    recurring = _print_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   297→    if not recurring and not completed and not resolved:
   298→        print(colorize("\n  First synthesis — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   299→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   300→        print(
   301→            colorize(
   302→                "  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?",
   303→                "dim",
   304→            )
   305→        )
   306→        print(colorize("  - Which findings will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   307→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   308→
   309→
   310→def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:
   311→    """Print completed cluster context for reflect stage."""
   312→    if not completed:
   313→        return
   314→    print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   315→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   316→        name = cluster.get("name", "?")
   317→        count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   318→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   319→        print(f"    {name}: {count} findings")
   320→        if thesis:
   321→            print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   322→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   323→            print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   324→    if len(completed) > 10:
   325→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   326→
   327→
   328→def _print_resolved_findings(resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   329→    """Print resolved findings delta since last synthesis."""
   330→    if not resolved:
   331→        return
   332→    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved findings since last synthesis: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   333→    for fid, finding in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   334→        status = finding.get("status", "")
   335→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   336→        detail = finding.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(finding.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   337→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   338→        print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   339→        print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   340→    if len(resolved) > 10:
   341→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   342→
   343→
   344→def _print_recurring_patterns(open_findings: dict, resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> bool:
   345→    """Print recurring pattern diagnostics for reflect stage."""
   346→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
   347→    if not recurring:
   348→        return False
   349→    print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   350→    for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   351→        resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   352→        open_count = len(info["open"])
   353→        label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   354→        print(
   355→            colorize(
   356→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   357→                "yellow",
   358→            )
   359→        )
   360→    return True
   361→
   362→

1→"""Handler for ``plan synthesize`` subcommand.
     2→
     3→Implements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
     4→with a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.
     5→
     6→Stage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:
     7→- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)
     8→- REFLECT: compare current findings against completed work (recurring patterns)
     9→- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps
    10→- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or "same"
    11→"""

    45→
    46→def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    47→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.
    48→
    49→    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    50→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    51→    """
    52→    if "::" in fid:
    53→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    54→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    55→            return suffix[:8]
    56→    return fid
    57→
    58→
    59→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    60→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    61→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    62→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    63→    for c in clusters.values():
    64→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    65→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    66→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    67→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    68→
    69→
    70→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    71→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment.
    72→
    73→    Returns list of (cluster_name, list_of_missing_fields).
    74→    Only checks non-auto clusters (manual clusters the agent creates).
    75→    """
    76→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    77→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    78→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    79→            continue
    80→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    81→            continue
    82→        missing: list[str] = []
    83→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    84→            missing.append("description")
    85→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
    86→            missing.append("action_steps")
    87→        if missing:
    88→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    89→    return gaps
    90→
    91→
    92→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    93→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
    94→    return [
    95→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    96→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
    97→    ]
    98→

   100→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   101→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   102→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
   103→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   104→        if stage_name in stages:

    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))

    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))

    32→def _plan_with_stages(*stage_names: str, confirmed: bool = False) -> dict:
    33→    """Build a plan with synthesis stages pre-recorded."""
    34→    plan = empty_plan()
    35→    plan["queue_order"] = [SYNTHESIS_ID]
    36→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    37→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
    38→    for name in stage_names:
    39→        stages[name] = {
    40→            "stage": name,
    41→            "report": f"A sufficiently long report for {name} stage that meets minimum length requirements and more text",
    42→            "cited_ids": [],
    43→            "timestamp": "2025-06-01T00:00:00Z",
    44→            "finding_count": 5,
    45→        }
    46→        if confirmed:
    47→            stages[name]["confirmed_at"] = "2025-06-01T00:01:00Z"
    48→            stages[name]["confirmed_text"] = "I have thoroughly reviewed all the findings in this stage"
    49→    return plan
    50→
    51→
    52→def _fake_runtime(state: dict):
    53→    """Build a minimal command_runtime return."""
    54→    return type("Ctx", (), {"state": state, "config": {}})()

    70→        "note": None,
    71→        "start": False,
    72→        "dry_run": False,
    73→    }
    74→    defaults.update(overrides)

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import re
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    17→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    18→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    19→    Cluster,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    synthesis_clusters,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    25→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    26→    review_finding_snapshot_hash,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)
   100→        if dim:
   101→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   102→
   103→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   104→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   105→        dim = _dimension(f)
   106→        if dim:
   107→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   108→
   109→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   111→        recurring[dim] = {
   112→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   113→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   114→        }
   115→    return recurring
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Data types
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→@dataclass
   123→class SynthesisInput:
   124→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
   125→
   126→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
   127→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
   128→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
   129→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
   130→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
   131→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
   132→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
   133→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
   134→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
   135→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last synthesis
   136→
   137→
   138→@dataclass
   139→class DismissedFinding:
   140→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
   141→
   142→    finding_id: str
   143→    reason: str
   144→
   145→
   146→@dataclass
   147→class ContradictionNote:
   148→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
   149→
   150→    kept: str
   151→    dismissed: str
   152→    reason: str
   153→
   154→
   155→@dataclass
   156→class SynthesisResult:
   157→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
   158→
   159→    strategy_summary: str
   160→    epics: list[dict]
   161→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
   162→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
   163→    priority_rationale: str = ""
   164→
   165→
   166→@dataclass
   167→class SynthesisMutationResult:
   168→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
   169→
   170→    epics_created: int = 0
   171→    epics_updated: int = 0
   172→    epics_completed: int = 0
   173→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
   174→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
   175→    strategy_summary: str = ""
   176→    synthesis_version: int = 0
   177→    dry_run: bool = False
   178→
   179→
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Input collection
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
   185→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   188→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   189→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   190→
   191→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   192→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   193→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   194→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   195→            continue
   196→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   197→            open_review[fid] = f
   198→        else:
   199→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   200→

   532→    save_plan(plan)

   574→# Completion

   576→
   577→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   578→    """Complete synthesis \u2014 requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   579→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)

   586→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})

   588→

   593→            print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   594→        else:
   595→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   596→            gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   597→            if gaps:
   598→                print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))

   604→                ))
   605→                print(colorize(

   607→                    "dim",
   608→                ))
   609→            else:
   610→                manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   611→                if manual:

   613→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   614→                else:

   616→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   617→            if meta.get("strategy_summary"):

   620→

   622→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   623→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --confirm organize", "dim"))
   624→        return
   625→
   626→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
   627→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
   628→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)

   632→            if c.get("finding_ids")
   633→        ]

   637→            return

## Synthesis Workflow Documentation

I've found and analyzed the complete synthesis workflow for agents in the Desloppify project. Here's what you need to know:

### Key Documentation Files

**Main SKILL.md files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/docs/SKILL.md` - Primary agent guide
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md` - Skill metadata version

These documents include a section on "Synthesis (after review)" that outlines the 4-stage workflow.

### 4-Stage Synthesis Workflow

The synthesis workflow is defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis_playbook.py`:

1. **OBSERVE** (Analyse findings & spot contradictions)
   - Agent analyzes themes and root causes
   - Minimum 100 characters (50 if ≤3 findings)
   - Command: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "analysis of themes and root causes..."`
   - Gate: No citation requirement—focuses on genuine analysis
   - Output: Records observation snapshot

2. **REFLECT** (Form strategy & present to user)
   - Compare current findings against previously completed work
   - Detect recurring patterns (resolved before, now open again)
   - Minimum 100 characters (50 if ≤3 findings)
   - Must mention at least one recurring dimension if patterns exist
   - Command: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."`
   - Gate: Validate against `completed_clusters` and `resolved_findings`
   - Output: Strategic briefing for user before organizing

3. **ORGANIZE** (Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize)
   - Create manual clusters grouping findings by root cause
   - Each manual cluster requires description + action_steps
   - Prioritize clusters with explicit ordering
   - Minimum 100 characters for report
   - Commands:
     ```bash
     desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."
     desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-patterns>
     desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"
     desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "summary of organization..."
     ```
   - Gate: Validates all manual clusters are enriched (description + steps)
   - Output: Prioritized organization for user

4. **COMMIT** (Write strategy & confirm)
   - Requires --strategy parameter (200+ chars or "same")
   - Removes `synthesis::pending` from queue
   - Marks synthesis complete
   - Command: `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "execution plan..."`
   - Gate: All clusters must be enriched; all or most findings clustered
   - Output: Ready for `desloppify next` to start implementation

### Dependencies Between Stages

```
observe → reflect → organize → commit
   ↓         ↓           ↓          ↓
  (no deps) observe   reflect    organize
```

Each stage depends on all prior stages being recorded.

### State Files Required

The `.desloppify` directory contains:

1. **plan.json** (17MB)
   - Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/plan.json`
   - Contains: `queue_order`, `clusters`, `skipped`, `epic_synthesis_meta`
   - Key fields for synthesis:
     - `epic_synthesis_meta`: Stores synthesis progress, stages, strategy
     - `clusters`: Cluster definitions with findings, descriptions, steps
     - `finding_snapshot_hash`: Detects if findings have changed

2. **state-python.json** (23MB)
   - Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/state-python.json`
   - Contains: All findings with their status, detector type, dimensions
   - Key for synthesis:
     - `findings`: All detected issues, including `review` and `concerns` detectors
     - `dimension_scores`: Design quality scores
     - `assessment_import_audit`: Review import history

3. **query.json** (3.4K)
   - Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/query.json`
   - Contains: Dimension prompts with descriptions and guidance
   - Defines what each dimension means (e.g., "initialization_coupling", "high_level_elegance")

4. **review_packet_blind.json** (327K)
   - Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json`
   - Used for subjective reviews by agents
   - Contains findings without score targets (blind to anchoring)

### Core Implementation Files

**Main handlers:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`
  - `_cmd_stage_observe()` - Records OBSERVE stage
  - `_cmd_stage_reflect()` - Records REFLECT stage (detects recurring patterns)
  - `_cmd_stage_organize()` - Records ORGANIZE stage (validates clusters)
  - `_cmd_synthesize_complete()` - Completes synthesis and removes pending

**Synthesis modules:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis/` (directory)
  - `shared.py` - Shared helpers, stage progress printing, cluster validation
  - `organize.py` - ORGANIZE stage validation (legacy, merged into handlers)
  - `reflect.py` - REFLECT stage (legacy, merged into handlers)
  - `__init__.py` - Empty module marker

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_synthesis.py`
  - `collect_synthesis_input()` - Gathers all data for synthesis
  - `detect_recurring_patterns()` - Finds dimensions with both resolved & open findings
  - `extract_finding_citations()` - Parses finding IDs from free text
  - `SynthesisInput` dataclass - Input structure
  - `SynthesisResult` dataclass - Output structure

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/synthesis.py`
  - Re-exports synthesis types for command layers

**Constants:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis_playbook.py`
  - `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS` - Display names for all 4 stages
  - `SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES` - DAG of stage dependencies
  - `SYNTH_CMD_*` - Canonical command snippets for agents

### Test Fixtures

**Synthesis-specific tests:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_epic_synthesis.py`
  - Tests input collection, snapshot hashing, stage progression
  - Helpers: `_state_with_review_findings()`, `_state_empty()`

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_synthesis_confirmation.py`
  - Tests stage confirmation workflow with attestation
  - Helpers: `_plan_with_stages()`, `_state_with_review_findings()`

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_synthesis_logging.py`
  - Tests synthesis logging and audit trails

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_synthesis_attestation.py`
  - Tests attestation requirements for stage confirmation

### Running the Synthesis Workflow

Typical agent flow:

```bash
# 1. After subjective review completes, OBSERVE is prompted
desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "I see 3 main themes:..."

# 2. Analyze previous work and form strategy
desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "Previous fixes held. Similar bugs recurring in..."

# 3. Create clusters (agent interaction with user)
desloppify plan cluster create "Import cycles" --description "Circular dependencies..."
desloppify plan cluster add "Import cycles" "structural::*.py"
desloppify plan cluster update "Import cycles" --steps "Break at boundary" "Add facade layer"

# 4. Record organization
desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "Prioritized 3 clusters. First: imports (unblocks others)..."

# 5. Complete synthesis
desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "Execute clusters in order: imports → tests → interfaces..."

# 6. Now work the queue
desloppify next
```

### Key Validation Gates

The synthesis workflow enforces several validation gates:

1. **Observe gate**: Requires 100+ char report (50+ if ≤3 findings)
2. **Reflect gate**: Requires report + mention of recurring dimensions (if any found)
3. **Organize gate**: All manual clusters must have description AND action_steps
4. **Commit gate**: All clusters enriched, findings clustered, 200+ char strategy (or "same")
5. **Cluster coverage**: Minimum organization before completion (unless 0 findings)

### Integration Points

- **After review**: `synthesis::pending` appears in queue when review findings need organization
- **Plan awareness**: Synthesis metadata stored in `plan.epic_synthesis_meta`
- **Finding snapshot**: Hash tracks if findings have changed since last synthesis
- **Next command**: After synthesis completes, `desloppify next` works clusters in priority order

This gives agents a structured, gated workflow for handling subjective review findings with clear confirmation points and cluster enrichment requirements.

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    57→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    58→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    59→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    60→```
    61→
    62→### Subjective reviews
    63→
    64→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    65→If you need to trigger one manually:
    66→```bash
    67→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    68→```
    69→
    70→### Other useful commands
    71→
    72→```bash
    73→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    74→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    75→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    76→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    77→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    78→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    79→```
    80→
    81→## 3. Reference
    82→
    83→### How scoring works
    84→
    85→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    86→
    87→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    88→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    89→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    90→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    91→
    92→### Subjective reviews in detail
    93→

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    57→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    58→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    59→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    60→```
    61→
    62→### Subjective reviews
    63→
    64→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    65→If you need to trigger one manually:
    66→```bash
    67→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    68→```
    69→
    70→### Other useful commands
    71→
    72→```bash
    73→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    74→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    75→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    76→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    77→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    78→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    79→```
    80→
    81→## 3. Reference
    82→
    83→### How scoring works
    84→
    85→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    86→
    87→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    88→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    89→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    90→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    91→
    92→### Subjective reviews in detail
    93→
    94→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
    95→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
    96→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    97→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
    98→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
    99→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   100→
   101→### Key concepts
   102→
   103→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   104→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   105→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   106→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   107→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   108→
   109→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   110→
   111→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   112→
   113→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   114→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   115→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.

usage: desloppify plan [-h] [--state STATE] [--output FILE]
                       {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster}
                       ...

Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.

positional arguments:
  {show,queue,reset,move,describe,note,skip,unskip,reopen,done,focus,cluster}
    show                Show plan metadata summary
    queue               Compact table of upcoming queue items
    reset               Reset plan to empty
    move                Move findings in the queue
    describe            Set augmented description
    note                Set note on findings
    skip                Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent
                        (wontfix), or --false-positive
    unskip              Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens
                        permanent/fp in state)
    reopen              Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue
    done                Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next
                        step)
    focus               Set or clear active cluster focus
    cluster             Manage finding clusters

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --state STATE         Path to state file
  --output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout

typical workflow:
  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed

subcommands:
  show       Show plan metadata summary
  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
  reset      Reset plan to empty
  move       Move findings in the queue
  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
  describe   Set augmented description
  note       Set note on findings
  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
  cluster    Manage finding clusters
```
