  1109→    *,
  1110→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
  1111→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
  1112→) -> str | None:

  1132→
  1133→    elif stage == "organize":
  1134→        if cluster_names:
  1135→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
  1136→            if not found:
  1137→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
  1138→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
  1139→

  1143→def _confirm_observe(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
  1144→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
  1145→    if "observe" not in stages:
  1146→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: observe stage not recorded.", "red"))
  1147→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
  1148→        return
  1149→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
  1150→        print(colorize("  Observe stage already confirmed.", "green"))
  1151→        return
  1152→
  1153→    # Show summary
  1154→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1155→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, runtime.state)
  1156→    obs = stages["observe"]
  1157→
  1158→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse findings & spot contradictions", "bold"))
  1159→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
  1160→
  1161→    # Dimension breakdown
  1162→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
  1163→    for _fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
  1164→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
  1165→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
  1166→        by_dim[dim] += 1
  1167→
  1168→    finding_count = obs.get("finding_count", len(si.open_findings))
  1169→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {finding_count} findings across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
  1170→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d)):
  1171→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} findings")
  1172→
  1173→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
  1174→    if cited:
  1175→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} finding IDs in your report.")
  1176→
  1177→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
  1178→
  1179→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
  1180→        if attestation:
  1181→            print(colorize(
  1182→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
  1183→                "red",
  1184→            ))
  1185→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
  1186→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
  1187→        print(colorize("  If not, continue reviewing findings before reflecting.", "dim"))
  1188→        return
  1189→
  1190→    # Validate attestation references actual data
  1191→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
  1192→    if validation_err:
  1193→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
  1194→        return
  1195→
  1196→    # Record confirmation
  1197→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
  1198→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
  1199→    save_plan(plan)

1364→def _cmd_synthesize_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1365→    """Manually inject ``synthesis::pending`` into the queue and clear prior stages."""
  1366→    plan = load_plan()
  1367→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
  1368→
  1369→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
  1370→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is already in the queue.", "yellow"))
  1371→        meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1372→        stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
  1373→        if stages:
  1374→            print(colorize(f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.", "yellow"))
  1375→            meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
  1376→            save_plan(plan)
  1377→            append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_start", actor="user",
  1378→                             detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())})
  1379→            save_plan(plan)
  1380→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
  1381→        else:
  1382→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
  1383→        print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
  1384→        return
  1385→
  1386→    # Inject at front
  1387→    order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
  1388→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1389→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
  1390→    save_plan(plan)
  1391→
  1392→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_start", actor="user",
  1393→                     detail={"action": "start"})
  1394→    save_plan(plan)
  1395→
  1396→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1397→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, runtime.state)
  1398→    print(colorize("  Synthesis started.", "green"))
  1399→    print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
  1400→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
  1401→    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
  1402→
  1403→
  1404→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1405→# Router
  1406→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1407→
  1408→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1409→    """Run epic synthesis: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
  1410→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1411→    state = runtime.state
  1412→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
  1413→        return

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   101→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   102→    if mechanical is not None:
   103→        return mechanical
   104→
   105→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   106→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   107→    if subjective is not None:
   108→        return subjective
   109→
   110→    # 4. Everything else
   111→    return ResolvedEntity(
   112→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   113→        pattern=pattern,
   114→        display_name=pattern,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   119→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   120→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   121→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   122→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   123→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   124→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   125→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   126→        if key not in lookup:
   127→            lookup[key] = detectors
   128→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   129→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   130→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   131→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   132→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   133→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   134→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   135→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   136→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   137→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   138→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   139→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   140→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   141→    return lookup
   142→
   143→
   144→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   145→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   146→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   147→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   148→
   149→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   150→    """
   151→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   152→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   153→    if not dim_data:
   154→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   155→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   156→                dim_data = ds_val
   157→                display_name = ds_key
   158→                break
   159→    return dim_data, display_name
   160→
   161→
   162→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   163→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   164→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   165→    if names:
   166→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   167→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   168→
   169→
   170→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   171→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   172→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   173→    pattern = args.pattern
   174→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   175→    if chronic:
   176→        scope = pattern
   177→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   178→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   179→    if not pattern:
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   187→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   188→
   189→
   190→def load_matches(
   191→    state: dict,
   192→    *,
   193→    scope: str | None,
   194→    status_filter: str,
   195→    chronic: bool,
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=None,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status_filter,
   205→            include_subjective=False,
   206→            chronic=chronic,
   207→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   208→        ),
   209→    )
   210→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   211→
   212→
   213→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
   214→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
   215→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   216→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   217→    )
   218→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   219→        matches,
   220→        budget=noise_budget,
   221→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   222→    )
   223→    return (
   224→        surfaced_matches,
   225→        hidden_by_detector,
   226→        noise_budget,
   227→        global_noise_budget,
   228→        budget_warning,
   229→    )
   230→
   231→
   232→__all__ = [
   233→    "ResolvedEntity",
   234→    "_detector_names_hint",
   235→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   236→    "load_matches",
   237→    "resolve_entity",
   238→    "resolve_noise",
   239→    "resolve_show_scope",
   240→]
   241→

1→"""Finding noise budget parsing and filtering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→__all__ = [
     6→    "DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET",
     7→    "DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET",
     8→    "resolve_finding_noise_budget",
     9→    "resolve_finding_noise_global_budget",
    10→    "resolve_finding_noise_settings",
    11→    "apply_finding_noise_budget",
    12→]
    13→
    14→DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET = 10
    15→DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET = 0
    16→_CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
    17→
    18→
    19→def _resolve_non_negative_int(raw_value: object, default: int) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    20→    """Parse a non-negative integer with fallback. Returns (value, was_valid)."""
    21→    try:
    22→        value = int(raw_value)
    23→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    24→        return default, False
    25→    if value < 0:
    26→        return 0, False
    27→    return value, True
    28→
    29→
    30→def resolve_finding_noise_budget(
    31→    config: dict | None, *, default: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET
    32→) -> int:
    33→    """Resolve per-detector noise budget from config with safe fallback."""
    34→    if not config:
    35→        return default
    36→    budget, _valid = _resolve_non_negative_int(
    37→        config.get("finding_noise_budget", default), default
    38→    )
    39→    return budget
    40→
    41→
    42→def resolve_finding_noise_global_budget(
    43→    config: dict | None, *, default: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET
    44→) -> int:
    45→    """Resolve global noise budget from config with safe fallback."""
    46→    if not config:
    47→        return default
    48→    budget, _valid = _resolve_non_negative_int(
    49→        config.get("finding_noise_global_budget", default),
    50→        default,
    51→    )
    52→    return budget
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_finding_noise_settings(
    56→    config: dict | None,
    57→    *,
    58→    per_default: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET,
    59→    global_default: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET,
    60→) -> tuple[int, int, str | None]:
    61→    """Resolve per-detector/global budgets and return an optional warning."""
    62→    if not config:
    63→        return per_default, global_default, None
    64→
    65→    per_value = config.get("finding_noise_budget", per_default)
    66→    global_value = config.get("finding_noise_global_budget", global_default)
    67→    per_budget, per_valid = _resolve_non_negative_int(per_value, per_default)
    68→    global_budget, global_valid = _resolve_non_negative_int(
    69→        global_value, global_default
    70→    )
    71→
    72→    warning_parts: list[str] = []
    73→    if not per_valid:
    74→        warning_parts.append(
    75→            f"Invalid config `finding_noise_budget={per_value!r}`; using {per_budget}"
    76→        )
    77→    if not global_valid:
    78→        warning_parts.append(
    79→            f"Invalid config `finding_noise_global_budget={global_value!r}`; using {global_budget}"
    80→        )
    81→    warning = " | ".join(warning_parts) if warning_parts else None
    82→    return per_budget, global_budget, warning
    83→
    84→
    85→def _finding_priority_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[int, int, str]:
    86→    """Sort by actionable priority (tier/confidence/id)."""
    87→    return (
    88→        finding.get("tier", 3),
    89→        _CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    90→        finding.get("id", ""),
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def _finding_display_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[str, int, int, str]:
    95→    """Sort by file/tier/confidence/id for deterministic display order."""
    96→    return (
    97→        finding.get("file", ""),
    98→        finding.get("tier", 3),
    99→        _CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   100→        finding.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def _group_findings_by_detector(findings: list[dict]) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   105→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   106→    for finding in findings:
   107→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   108→        grouped.setdefault(detector, []).append(finding)
   109→    return grouped
   110→
   111→
   112→def _cap_detector_groups(
   113→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]], budget: int
   114→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[dict]], dict[str, int]]:
   115→    capped_by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   116→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   117→
   118→    for detector, detector_findings in grouped.items():
   119→        detector_findings.sort([REDACTED])
   120→        capped = detector_findings if budget <= 0 else detector_findings[:budget]
   121→        capped_by_detector[detector] = list(capped)
   122→        hidden_count = max(0, len(detector_findings) - len(capped))
   123→        if hidden_count:
   124→            hidden_by_detector[detector] = hidden_count
   125→
   126→    return capped_by_detector, hidden_by_detector
   127→
   128→
   129→def _round_robin_global_budget(
   130→    capped_by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]], global_budget: int
   131→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   132→    surfaced: list[dict] = []
   133→    hidden_after_global: dict[str, int] = {}
   134→
   135→    detector_order = sorted(
   136→        capped_by_detector.keys(),
   137→        key=lambda detector: (
   138→            _finding_priority_key(capped_by_detector[detector][0])
   139→            if capped_by_detector[detector]
   140→            else (9, 9, ""),
   141→            detector,
   142→        ),
   143→    )
   144→    consumed: dict[str, int] = {detector: 0 for detector in detector_order}
   145→
   146→    while len(surfaced) < global_budget:
   147→        progressed = False
   148→        for detector in detector_order:
   149→            idx = consumed[detector]
   150→            detector_items = capped_by_detector[detector]
   151→            if idx >= len(detector_items):
   152→                continue
   153→            surfaced.append(detector_items[idx])
   154→            consumed[detector] = idx + 1
   155→            progressed = True
   156→            if len(surfaced) >= global_budget:
   157→                break
   158→        if not progressed:
   159→            break
   160→
   161→    for detector, detector_items in capped_by_detector.items():
   162→        dropped = len(detector_items) - consumed.get(detector, 0)
   163→        if dropped > 0:
   164→            hidden_after_global[detector] = dropped
   165→
   166→    return surfaced, hidden_after_global
   167→
   168→
   169→def _sort_hidden_counts(hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> dict[str, int]:
   170→    return dict(
   171→        sorted(hidden_by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]))
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→def apply_finding_noise_budget(
   176→    findings: list[dict],
   177→    budget: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET,
   178→    global_budget: int = DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET,
   179→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   180→    """Cap surfaced findings per detector and return hidden counts."""
   181→    if budget <= 0 and global_budget <= 0:
   182→        return list(findings), {}
   183→
   184→    grouped = _group_findings_by_detector(findings)
   185→    capped_by_detector, hidden_by_detector = _cap_detector_groups(grouped, budget)
   186→
   187→    if global_budget > 0:
   188→        surfaced, hidden_after_global = _round_robin_global_budget(
   189→            capped_by_detector,
   190→            global_budget,
   191→        )
   192→        for detector, count in hidden_after_global.items():
   193→            hidden_by_detector[detector] = hidden_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + count
   194→    else:
   195→        surfaced = [item for items in capped_by_detector.values() for item in items]
   196→
   197→    surfaced.sort([REDACTED])
   198→    return surfaced, _sort_hidden_counts(hidden_by_detector)
   199→

1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import (
     9→    print_agent_plan as render_plan_with_living_plan,
    10→    print_ranked_actions,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    14→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    18→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    19→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    20→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    21→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    22→
    23→from .formatting import format_detail
    24→
    25→
    26→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    27→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    28→    try:
    29→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    30→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    31→    except OSError as exc:
    32→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    33→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    34→        return False
    35→    return True
    36→
    37→
    38→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    39→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    40→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    41→    for finding in matches:
    42→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    43→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    47→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    48→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    49→    status_icon = {
    50→        "open": "○",
    51→        "fixed": "✓",
    52→        "wontfix": "—",
    53→        "false_positive": "✗",
    54→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    55→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    56→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    57→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    58→    print(
    59→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    63→    if detail_parts:
    64→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    65→    if show_code:
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    67→        target_line = (
    68→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    69→        )
    70→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    71→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    72→            if snippet:
    73→                print(snippet)
    74→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    75→        print(
    76→            colorize(
    77→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    78→                "red",
    79→            )
    80→        )
    81→    if finding.get("note"):
    82→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    83→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    84→
    85→
    86→def render_findings(
    87→    matches: list[dict],
    88→    *,
    89→    pattern: str,
    90→    status_filter: str,
    91→    show_code: bool,
    92→    top: int,
    93→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    hidden_total: int,
    95→    noise_budget: int,
    96→    global_noise_budget: int,
    97→    budget_warning: str | None,
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
   100→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
   101→    print(
   102→        colorize(
   103→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   104→            "bold",
   105→        )
   106→    )
   107→    if budget_warning:
   108→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   109→    if hidden_total:
   110→        global_label = (
   111→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   112→        )
   113→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   114→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   115→        )
   116→        print(
   117→            colorize(
   118→                (
   119→                    "  Noise budget: "
   120→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   121→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   122→                ),
   123→                "dim",
   124→            )
   125→        )
   126→
   127→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   128→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   129→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   130→
   131→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   132→        findings.sort(
   133→            key=lambda finding: (
   134→                finding["tier"],
   135→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   136→            )
   137→        )
   138→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   139→        print(
   140→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   141→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   142→        )
   143→
   144→        for finding in findings:
   145→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   146→        print()
   147→
   148→    if remaining_findings:
   149→        print(
   150→            colorize(
   151→                (
   152→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   153→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   154→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   155→                ),
   156→                "dim",
   157→            )
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   161→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   162→    for finding in matches:
   163→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   164→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   165→
   166→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   167→    print(
   168→        colorize(
   169→            (
   170→                "    By tier:     "
   171→                + ", ".join(
   172→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   173→                )
   174→            ),
   175→            "dim",
   176→        )
   177→    )
   178→    print(
   179→        colorize(
   180→            (
   181→                "    By detector: "
   182→                + ", ".join(
   183→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   184→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   185→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   186→                    )
   187→                )
   188→            ),
   189→            "dim",
   190→        )
   191→    )
   192→    if hidden_total:
   193→        print(
   194→            colorize(
   195→                (
   196→                    "    Hidden:      "
   197→                    + ", ".join(
   198→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   199→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   200→                    )
   201→                ),
   202→                "dim",
   203→            )
   204→        )
   205→    print()
   206→
   207→
   208→def show_agent_plan(
   209→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   210→) -> None:
   211→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions.
   212→
   213→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   214→    """
   215→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
   216→        render_plan_with_living_plan(
   217→            [],
   218→            plan=plan,
   219→            header="  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next` to see your next task):",
   220→        )

   316→def create_cluster(
   317→    plan: PlanModel,
   318→    name: str,
   319→    description: str | None = None,
   320→    action: str | None = None,
   321→) -> Cluster:
   322→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   323→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   324→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   325→        raise ValueError(
   326→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   327→        )
   328→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   329→        raise ValueError(
   330→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis epics: {name!r}"
   331→        )
   332→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   333→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   334→    now = utc_now()
   335→    cluster: Cluster = {
   336→        "name": name,
   337→        "description": description,
   338→        "finding_ids": [],
   339→        "created_at": now,
   340→        "updated_at": now,
   341→        "auto": False,
   342→        "cluster_key": "",
   343→        "action": action,
   344→        "user_modified": False,
   345→    }
   346→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   347→    return cluster
   348→
   349→
   350→def add_to_cluster(
   351→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   352→) -> int:
   353→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   354→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   355→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   356→    if cluster is None:
   357→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   358→
   359→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   360→    count = 0
   361→    now = utc_now()
   362→    for fid in finding_ids:
   363→        if fid not in member_ids:
   364→            member_ids.append(fid)
   365→            count += 1
   366→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   367→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   368→        if fid not in overrides:
   369→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   370→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   371→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   372→
   373→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   374→    return count
   375→
   376→
   377→def remove_from_cluster(
   378→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   379→) -> int:
   380→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   381→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   382→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   383→    if cluster is None:
   384→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   385→
   386→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   387→    now = utc_now()
   388→    count = 0
   389→    for fid in finding_ids:
   390→        if fid in member_ids:
   391→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   392→            count += 1
   393→        # Clear cluster from override
   394→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   395→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   396→            override["cluster"] = None
   397→            override["updated_at"] = now
   398→
   399→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   400→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   401→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   402→
   403→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   404→    return count
   405→
   406→
   407→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   409→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   410→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   411→    if cluster is None:
   412→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   413→
   414→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   415→    now = utc_now()
   416→    for fid in orphaned:
   417→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   418→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   419→            override["cluster"] = None
   420→            override["updated_at"] = now
   421→
   422→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   423→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   424→
   425→    return orphaned
   426→
   427→
   428→def move_cluster(
   429→    plan: PlanModel,
   430→    cluster_name: str,
   431→    position: str,
   432→    target: str | None = None,
   433→    offset: int | None = None,
   434→) -> int:
   435→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   436→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   437→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   438→    if cluster is None:
   439→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   440→
   441→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   442→    if not member_ids:
   443→        return 0
   444→
   445→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   446→
   447→
   448→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   449→# Focus
   450→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   451→
   452→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   453→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   454→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   455→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   456→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   457→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   458→
   459→
   460→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   461→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   462→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   463→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   464→
   465→
   466→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   467→# Reset
   468→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   469→
   470→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   471→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   472→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   473→    plan.clear()
   474→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   475→        plan[k] = v
   476→    plan["created"] = created
   477→
   478→
   479→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   480→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   481→
   482→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   483→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   484→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   485→    """
   486→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   487→    found = 0
   488→
   489→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   490→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   491→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   492→
   493→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   494→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   495→    for fid in finding_ids:
   496→        was_present = False
   497→        if fid in order:
   498→            order.remove(fid)
   499→            was_present = True

## Comprehensive Code Path Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the five areas you requested. Here are the exact findings:

### 1. **`--start` Auto-Start Synthesis**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`

**Main Function:** `_cmd_synthesize_start()` (Lines 1364-1401)
- **Lines 1415-1418:** Router in `cmd_plan_synthesize()` checks `if getattr(args, "start", False)` and routes to `_cmd_synthesize_start()`
- **Lines 1369-1370:** If `synthesis::pending` (constant `SYNTHESIS_ID` from `stale_dimensions.py` line 20) is already in queue, shows message and optionally clears prior stages (lines 1373-1379)
- **Lines 1386-1387:** If not in queue, injects at front: `order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)`
- **Lines 1388-1389:** Clears stages: `meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}`
- **Lines 1396-1401:** Prints confirmation with open review finding count

**--stage observe Check:** 
- **Lines 226-228 in `_cmd_stage_observe()`:** Checks `if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", [])` and rejects with "synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to observe."
- This is the dependency check for all observation attempts

---

### 2. **`plan skip` Suffix Matching Chain**

**File Chain:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/_resolve.py` → `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` → `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py`

**Resolution Path:**
1. **`_resolve.py` lines 9-45:** `resolve_ids_from_patterns()` function:
   - Line 34: Calls `match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)` for each pattern
   - Lines 27-31: Also collects all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
   - Lines 41-44: Falls back to plan IDs if pattern doesn't match state findings

2. **`resolution.py` lines 83-94:** `match_findings()` function:
   - Line 93: Uses `_matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)` to test each finding

3. **`filtering.py` lines 176-190:** `_matches_pattern()` function (the pattern matcher):
   - **Line 178:** Exact match: `if finding_id == pattern`
   - **Line 180:** Glob match: `if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern)`
   - **Line 182:** Prefix match: `if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern)`
   - **Lines 184-185:** **SUFFIX MATCH** (newly added): `if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern)` — matches 8-hex-digit suffixes like `::abc12345`
   - **Lines 187-190:** Detector/file matching

**Plan Queue Search:** 
- **`override_handlers.py` lines 185, 250:** `resolve_ids_from_patterns()` is called with `plan=plan` argument, which enables the plan literal-match fallback (lines 41-44 in `_resolve.py`)
- No direct queue_order search happens; plan IDs are collected as fallback when state matching fails

---

### 3. **Noise Budget in `show` Command**

**Default Budget:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/noise.py`
- **Line 14:** `DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_BUDGET = 10` (10 per detector)
- **Line 15:** `DEFAULT_FINDING_NOISE_GLOBAL_BUDGET = 0` (disabled by default)

**Budget Resolution:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/noise.py` lines 55-82
- **Function:** `resolve_finding_noise_settings(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, int, str | None]`
- Reads from `config.get("finding_noise_budget", 10)` and `config.get("finding_noise_global_budget", 0)`
- Returns tuple: `(per_detector_budget, global_budget, optional_warning)`

**Application in `show`:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`
- **Lines 268-271:** Calls `resolve_noise(config, matches)` which internally calls `state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)`
- **Lines 268-271:** Returns `(surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector, noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning)`

**`--top` Interaction:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py` lines 86-158
- **Line 92:** `top: int` parameter is passed separately
- **Lines 127-128:** Applies after noise budget: `shown_files = sorted_files[:top]` — slices file count, NOT finding count
- **Line 127-158:** The `--top` flag controls which files to display (limits file display), AFTER noise budget filters individual findings per detector
- **Example:** `--top 20` shows first 20 files, but within each file, noise budget caps detectors independently (10 findings per detector by default)

---

### 4. **Confirmation Flow (7-Step Synthesis Journey)**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`

**Stage Dependencies:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesis_playbook.py` lines 12-17
```python
SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    "observe": set(),
    "reflect": {"observe"},
    "organize": {"reflect"},
    "commit": {"organize"},
}
```

**7-Step Flow:**

1. **OBSERVE (Lines 208-280):** Record analysis report
   - **Checks:** 
     - Line 226: `SYNTHESIS_ID not in queue_order` → block with message
     - Line 250: Min 100 chars (50 if ≤3 findings)
   - **Saves:** `stages["observe"]` with report, cited_ids, timestamp
   - **Prompt:** "Now confirm your analysis" (line 278)

2. **CONFIRM OBSERVE (Lines 1143-1203):** Attest to observation
   - **Function:** `_confirm_observe()` (lines 1143-1203)
   - **Checks:**
     - Line 1145: `"observe" not in stages` → block
     - Line 1149: `stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at")` → skip if already confirmed
     - Line 1179: Attestation min 80 chars (`_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN` line 1103)
     - Line 1191: Validate attestation references dimension names
   - **Saves:** `stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"]` = utc_now()
   - **Logs:** `synthesis_confirm_observe` entry

3. **REFLECT (Lines 282-375):** Compare findings vs completed work
   - **Checks:**
     - Line 304: `SYNTHESIS_ID not in queue_order` → block
     - Line 310: `"observe" not in stages` → block with message (line 311-312)
     - **Line 314-316:** `if not stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at")` → block: "observe stage not confirmed"
     - Line 324: Min 100 chars (50 if ≤3 findings)
     - Lines 334-349: If recurring patterns detected, report must mention at least one dimension name
   - **Saves:** `stages["reflect"]` with report, detected patterns, timestamp
   - **Prompt:** "Now confirm your strategy" (line 374)

4. **CONFIRM REFLECT (Lines 1206-1276):** Attest to strategy
   - **Function:** `_confirm_reflect()` (lines 1206-1276)
   - **Checks:**
     - Line 1208: `"reflect" not in stages` → block
     - Line 1212: Already confirmed check
     - Line 1179: Attestation min 80 chars
     - Line 1191: Validate attestation references dimension or cluster names
   - **Saves:** `stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"]` = utc_now()
   - **Logs:** `synthesis_confirm_reflect` entry

5. **ORGANIZE (Lines 422-530):** Create/enrich clusters & set queue order
   - **Checks:**
     - Line 456: `SYNTHESIS_ID not in queue_order` → block
     - **Lines 457-460:** `if not stages["reflect"].get("confirmed_at")` → block: "reflect stage not confirmed"
     - Lines 462-488: Validate manual clusters have description + action_steps
   - **Saves:** Creates/updates clusters with action_steps, sets `stages["organize"]`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`

**Available Primitives (NO explicit merge function exists):**

2. **`add_to_cluster()`** (lines 350-374)

   - Updates overrides to track cluster membership

4. **`delete_cluster()`** (lines 407-425)

5. **`move_cluster()`** (lines 428-445)

   - Moves all cluster members as contiguous block in queue_order
   - Delegates to `move_items()`

**Manual Cluster Merge Pattern (no built-in function):**
To merge cluster A into cluster B:
1. Get member IDs: `cluster_a_ids = plan["clusters"]["A"]["finding_ids"]`
2. Add to target: `add_to_cluster(plan, "B", cluster_a_ids)`
3. Delete source: `delete_cluster(plan, "A")`
4. Manually merge metadata (description, action_steps) if desired
5. Save plan

**Exported Functions:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan.py` lines 37-54
- All operations are exported via `plan.py` public API

## Summary of Key Insights

| Area | Key Finding |
|------|-------------|
| **Auto-start** | `--start` flag injects `synthesis::pending` at queue front and clears all prior stages; all subsequent `--stage` commands check that `SYNTHESIS_ID` is in queue_order before proceeding |
| **Pattern Matching** | `plan skip` → `resolve_ids_from_patterns()` → `match_findings()` → `_matches_pattern()` chain. Suffix matching (line 184-185) supports 8-digit hex IDs as `::abc12345` |
| **Noise Budget** | Default 10/detector + 0 global; applied BEFORE `--top` flag; `--top` controls file display count only, not finding count per detector |
| **Confirmation** | Full 7-step flow with dependency gates: observe→confirm→reflect→confirm→organize→confirm→complete; each stage validates prior stage confirmation |
| **Cluster Merge** | No built-in merge; use primitives: `add_to_cluster()` + `delete_cluster()` to manually combine clusters |

     ```python
     import re
     _HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')

     def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
         """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
         if finding_id == pattern:
             return True
         if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
             return True
         if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
             return True
         if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
             return True
         return (
             finding.get("detector") == pattern
             or finding["file"] == pattern
             or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
         )
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`** — Main synthesis workflow handlers

     ```python
     def _validate_attestation(
         attestation: str,
         stage: str,
         *,
         dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
         cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
     ) -> str | None:
         """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
         text = attestation.lower()
         if stage == "observe":
             if dimensions:
                 found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
                 if not found:
                     dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
                     return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
         elif stage == "reflect":
             refs: list[str] = []
             if dimensions:
                 refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
             if cluster_names:
                 refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
             if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
                 hints = (dimensions or [])[:4] + (cluster_names or [])[:4]
                 return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster. Mention one of: {', '.join(hints)}"
         elif stage == "organize":
             if cluster_names:
                 found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
                 if not found:
                     names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
                     return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
         return None
     ```
     - Wired validation into `_confirm_observe()` (collecting dim_names from by_dim), `_confirm_reflect()` (collecting reflect_dims + reflect_clusters), `_confirm_organize()` (collecting organize_clusters)

     # Check for overlap with other manual clusters
